BuzzCatz Coffee & Sweets is located in the heart of Orange Beach on Canal Road in Cosmo's Courtyard. Owners Brian and Jodi Harsany's attention to detail and love of dining has created something truly memorable that has become a favorite for locals and visitors alike. Chef Kimberly Asbury, Pastry Chef and General Manager, has been working in the restaurant industry for over 20 years from small bagel shops and pizza places to fine dining Italian restaurants. Much of her knowledge has been gained through hands-on experience, but in 2003 she graduated from Penn College with a degree in baking and pastry.
